1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the nonlinear structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 850. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 507 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the nonlinear decomposition. By minimality, the element a^850 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 507-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
